the next implementation boundary is language/rendering consolidation on
the verified v4 substrate, not another architecture expansion.

# appendix e --- current structure v4.0.0 source snapshot

The following supplied structure document is retained verbatim for
lineage and cross-reference. Its inclusion does not change its authority
class.

# savant structure

**document revision:** 4.0.0\
**date:** 2026-10-01\
**document kind:** authority-aware structural constitution projection
and implementation map\
**runtime root:** `/root/savant-runtime`\
**predecessor:** `savant_structure_current_v3.31.0.md`\
**companion:** `savant_masterplan_v4.0.0.md`\
**authority effect:** none

> this document records the strongest current structure justified by
> authority and verified implementation. it does not create authority.
> unresolved placement or ownership remains unresolved.

## 1. structural objective

savant is reusable semantic substance rather than duplicated
application-local objects.

`substantiate once → instance → compose → relate through typed segues → project → execute → record → replay`

authority, truth, provenance, lineage, dependencies, security, and
recovery cut across this structure.

## 2. primary edifices

exactly three current primary edifices:

``` text
/root/savant-runtime/edifices/
├── identity/
├── utility/
└── programming/
```

utility:

``` text
glyph → iota → spasm → vagary → epiphany → tactic → artifice → agenda → oeuvre
```

programming:

``` text
glyph → line → segment → snippet → script → engine → subsystem → system → application
```

the identity edifice exists. its complete accepted ladder is unknown
from the evidence used here and is not inferred from symmetry.

## 3. canonical semantic identity

the lexicon is the semantic identity boundary.

``` text
truth          → lex:core:truth
determinism    → lex:core:determinism
relationship   → lex:core:kindred
segue          → lex:core:segue
provenance     → lex:core:provenance
lineage        → lex:core:lineage
authority      → lex:core:authority
evidence       → lex:primitive:evidence
canon          → lex:primitive:canon
runtime        → lex:implementation:igneo
projection     → lex:core:wavre
instance       → lex:core:shade
interface      → lex:implementation:inface
graph          → lex:implementation:gridd
```

active structured elements reference these identities rather than
copying/redefining them.

## 4. truth structure

``` text
truth
├── unknown
├── claimed
├── supported
├── contested
├── contradicted
└── verified
```

truth state cannot self-promote. authority and truth remain separate
axes. deterministic derivation is not verification.

## 5. runtime shard model

current directive makes shards the runtime form into which savant
programs lower.

``` text
canonical substance
      │
      ├── typed shard instance
      ├── typed shard instance
      └── typed shard instance
              │
              └── typed segue / segue shard
                         │
                         ▼
                    composition
                         │
                         ▼
                     projection
                         │
                         ▼
                      runtime
```

requirements:

-   shard identity exposes canonical source identity;
-   shard types are typed rather than free-form labels;
-   segue shards bind to canonical Kindred/Segue relationship identity;
-   runtime shard creation does not duplicate authoritative substance;
-   compositions expose dependencies/dependents;
-   runtime state remains non-authoritative unless an accepted owner
    admits/mutates it;
-   replay reconstructs derived shard arrangements where claimed.

the complete shard taxonomy is not frozen.

## 6. ownership map

``` text
lexicon
└── canonical semantic identity

kindred
└── relationship methodology/system

segue
└── individual typed relationship/transition

coda
└── authorized durable mutation
    ├── optimistic concurrency
    ├── atomic filesystem mutation
    ├── content-addressed backups
    ├── receipts
    └── rollback

vault
└── custody / retention / replay / recovery

pryme
└── authority interpretation

lythe
└── deterministic derivation

filament
└── projection execution / workers

dryve
└── delegated action substrate

carbon
└── straub
    └── datrix
```

this map does not claim every owner is fully implemented or that
unresolved faculty purposes are settled.

## 7. datrix

``` text
datrix
├── canonical registry substance
├── instances
├── umbra metadata
├── membrane typed relationships
├── dyad projections
├── composition / groups
├── immutable history
├── query / dependency projections
├── function-step / execution records
└── isotope
    └── deterministic non-authoritative projection
```

rules:

-   carbon owns straub;
-   straub creates/operates datrices;
-   straub is not a datrix;
-   `radia` is absent from active semantics;
-   no physical database engine is currently authoritative;
-   isotopes are derived/rebuildable;
-   mutation is immutable semantic evolution;
-   coda owns durable commit;
-   history survives current-view changes.

## 8. datrix evolution topology

``` text
subject v1
   │
   ├── superseded by ──► revision v2
   │                         │
   │                         └── patch substance
   │
   └── history retains both

revision v2
   │
   └── retired by ──► retirement marker

current isotope
   └── projects effective non-retired state
```

durable transaction:

``` text
nimble/translucent intent
→ validated semantic operations
→ staged immutable datrix evolution
→ canonical capsule
→ coda commit
→ receipt / backup
→ reopen / current projection
```

## 9. translucent / lucid

``` text
translucent
├── language semantics
├── compiler
├── normalized ir
├── shard lowering
├── execution graph
├── projection interfaces
└── lucid source representation
```

primary extension: `.lucid`.

``` text
program.lucid
→ lucid parser
→ translucent ast
→ normalized translucent ir
→ canonical lexeme references
→ truth/provenance/lineage/dependency bindings
→ shard lowering
→ execution graph
→ datrix/nimble, blot., or other savant executor
```

lucid is not a JavaScript wrapper. source syntax should make
composition, canonical identity, shards, and typed relationships
first-class rather than library calls attached to another language.

## 10. nimble

``` text
nimble
├── parser
├── datrix semantic ast
├── normalized datrix plan
├── truth/provenance/lineage constraints
├── graph/composition query
├── immutable evolution
├── isotope projection
└── coda commit adapter
```

primary extension: `.nimble`.

``` text
program.nimble
→ parse
→ semantic plan
→ resolve datrix/profile
→ resolve canonical identities
→ query / traverse / compose / evolve
→ project current/history/isotope
→ coda if durable mutation is required
→ receipt
```

nimble is not SQL with cosmetic syntax. SQL adapters, if justified,
terminate outside this semantic core.

## 11. blot.

canonical display name: `blot.`

always lowercase with terminal period in prose/UI. filesystem/import
identifiers may omit punctuation only when host syntax requires it.

``` text
blot.
├── visual semantic graph
│   ├── geometry shards
│   ├── style shards
│   ├── topology shards
│   ├── constraint shards
│   ├── motif instances
│   └── typed visual segues
├── geometry kernel
│   ├── paths
│   ├── curves
│   ├── contours
│   ├── transforms
│   ├── booleans
│   └── constraints
├── composition
│   ├── symbols
│   ├── instances
│   ├── patterns
│   ├── masks
│   ├── clips
│   ├── gradients
│   └── filters
├── solver
│   ├── dependency ordering
│   ├── constraint solving
│   ├── topology validation
│   └── deterministic regeneration
├── optimizer
│   ├── canonical path normalization
│   ├── deduplication
│   ├── symbol extraction
│   ├── simplification
│   └── stable ids
└── svg projection
    ├── safe svg
    ├── metadata
    ├── source maps
    └── accessibility projection
```

blot.'s compression law:

`substantiate geometry once → instance → parameterize → relate with typed constraints → compose → project svg`

complexity grows through composition rather than repeated path literals.

## 12. canonical geometry

a rendered svg path is normally a projection, not canonical geometry.

canonical blot. geometry retains enough semantic structure for parameter
changes, deterministic regeneration, alternate projections, topology
checks, source mapping, deduplication, motif reuse, and precision
changes without losing identity.

## 13. blot. safety

default svg projection rejects or constrains:

-   scripts;
-   event handlers;
-   unsafe external references;
-   unresolved internal references;
-   cyclic use expansion;
-   unbounded recursion;
-   unbounded element/path/coordinate counts;
-   foreign executable namespaces.

presentation remains non-authoritative.

## 14. file formats

current first-class source formats:

``` text
*.lucid   → translucent source
*.nimble  → nimble datrix source
*.svg     → external/interchange projection emitted by blot.
```

no additional first-class source extensions are currently justified.

compiled ir, plans, isotopes, receipts, indexes, caches, debug views,
source-map indexes, and optimized svg are projections/runtime artifacts
unless a distinct durable lifecycle or interoperability contract later
proves otherwise.

## 15. language relationship

``` text
                    savant semantic substrate
                             │
              ┌──────────────┴──────────────┐
              │                             │
         translucent                     nimble
         via .lucid                  via .nimble
              │                             │
      general savant programs          datrix programs
              │                             │
              ├──────────────┬──────────────┤
              │              │              │
             shards       typed segues    truth
              │              │              │
              └──────────────┴──────────────┘
                             │
                        execution
                             │
                 ┌───────────┴───────────┐
                 │                       │
               blot.                   datrix
                 │                       │
               svg                     coda
```

this is a target composition map, not proof every arrow is implemented.

## 16. verified implementation boundary

live-verified:

-   truth/determinism lexicon migration;
-   semantic identity/truth selftest;
-   translucent compiler v3;
-   hardened translucent svg runtime v3;
-   datrix/translucent v3;
-   coda mutation runtime;
-   datrix immutable mutation/transaction v4.

not live-verified:

-   attempted v5 datrix replacement, because installed
    `runtime.translucent.datrix_language` lacked the expected
    `selftest`;
-   native `.lucid` syntax under the current definition;
-   native `.nimble` syntax under the current definition;
-   dedicated blot. implementation;
-   complete shard lowering.

## 17. compatibility

evolution proceeds through adapters/lowering rather than destructive
replacement.

-   retain current translucent svg runtime as compatibility substrate
    until blot. owns advanced rendering and callers are preserved.
-   retain datrix v4 semantics while nimble becomes the native source
    language.
-   normalize external svg into blot. primitives where
    composition/editing is required.
-   if SQL interoperability is required:
    `sql → adapter → validated nimble/datrix semantic plan`.
-   if browser JavaScript is required, generated JavaScript is a
    deployment projection, not translucent identity.

## 18. persistence and projections

authoritative primitives and immutable history are durable.

rebuildable derived structures include, where practical:

-   indexes;
-   query projections;
-   isotopes;
-   compiled lucid ir;
-   compiled nimble plans;
-   blot. optimized svg;
-   caches;
-   debug views;
-   source-map indexes.

cache loss must not destroy semantic meaning.

## 19. provenance and lineage

substantial compilation/evolution paths preserve:

``` text
source identity
→ source digest
→ parser/compiler version
→ canonical semantic identities
→ normalized ir/plan digest
→ dependencies
→ lineage
→ provenance
→ execution/projection digest
→ receipt where durable mutation occurs
```

incidental process state does not contaminate a digest claiming semantic
determinism.

## 20. dependencies

dependencies are explicit and directional.

higher compositions depend on lower substance without owning/copying it.

legitimate recursive models require explicit bounded semantics. hidden
import cycles are not a relationship model.

## 21. security

-   strict input boundaries;
-   path containment for filesystem mutation;
-   coda remains mutation owner;
-   external protocols normalize at adapters;
-   external svg is untrusted input;
-   lucid/nimble source is parsed, never evaluated as host-language
    source;
-   resource limits cover parser depth, traversal, query expansion,
    geometry complexity, and rendering;
-   secrets stay outside semantic artifacts;
-   no renderer/front end gains authority by executing.

## 22. determinism

a component may claim deterministic semantic behavior only when
equivalent admitted semantic inputs under the same compatible
rules/schema/algorithm version produce the same semantic result.

exclude incidental values such as wall-clock time unless explicit input,
temporary paths, process ids, cache location, nondeterministic iteration
order, physical receipt timestamps from semantic digests, and
presentation-only changes where the digest claims semantic identity.

## 23. unresolved structural questions

preserve as unresolved:

-   final straub physical storage engine;
-   datrix allocation/splitting/reference policy;
-   distributed consensus need;
-   complete shard type catalog;
-   final ownership placement of blot. unless stronger accepted
    authority exists;
-   final lucid grammar;
-   final nimble grammar;
-   need for additional source extensions;
-   unresolved faculty/exile purposes;
-   atlas/scyon ownership where authority is incomplete.

## 24. structural completion condition

savant structure is complete only when:

-   every accepted semantic capability has one owner;
-   every authoritative primitive is substantiated once;
-   contextual uses are instances;
-   higher capability emerges through composition;
-   relationships are typed segues;
-   active reserved concepts resolve to canonical semantic identities;
-   projections are deterministic/rebuildable where claimed;
-   truth cannot self-promote;
-   runtime consequence cannot silently become authority;
-   durable mutation ownership is enforced;
-   lineage/provenance survive evolution;
-   dependencies/dependents are visible;
-   persistence/recovery semantics are explicit;
-   external interfaces normalize at boundaries;
-   lucid and nimble remain savant-native rather than host-language
    disguises;
-   blot. can express complex svg through reusable modular geometry;
-   unresolved authority remains visible;
-   evolution does not require duplicated truth.

# deferred future implementation register

## purpose and boundary

this register preserves accepted implementations that are intentionally
deferred until the basic savant implementation is complete.

an entry in this register is not evidence that the capability is
implemented, live-verified, activated, or part of the current completion
boundary. it is a preserved future implementation directive. deferred
work must not displace, expand, or obscure the immediate basic-savant
completion path.

future implementations remain subject to the authority model, accepted
decisions, compatibility requirements, lineage, provenance,
deterministic projection rules, ownership boundaries, and implementation
evidence in force when they are eventually activated.

the register is append-only in meaning: an accepted entry may be
superseded by later authority, but should not silently disappear.
proposals that have not been accepted do not enter this register as
accepted future implementations.

## future-001 --- living doctrine atlas

**status:** accepted future implementation\
**priority:** deferred\
**activation boundary:** after basic savant is complete\
**authority effect:** none until separately implemented and admitted
through normal savant authority and evidence processes

the master doctrine viewer should eventually evolve into a living,
deterministic, interactive visual atlas of savant while preserving the
complete textual doctrine as the governing readable projection.

the atlas is a projection layer, not a second source of authority.

its durable kernel is:

``` text
authoritative savant substance
→ authority and source resolution
→ canonical visual semantics
→ visual instances and typed relationships
→ deterministic visualization selection
→ layout and geometry projection
→ svg interaction and semantic motion
→ living doctrine atlas
```

the future implementation should preserve and extend the existing
viewer's valid reading, search, navigation, focus, telemetry, responsive
behavior, and source-digest behavior rather than replacing them merely
for presentation.

### semantic visual projection

visualizations should be selected from the semantics of the source
material rather than forced into a uniform diagram template.

supported visual grammars may include, where justified by source
semantics:

-   orthogonal coordinate systems;
-   semantic constellations;
-   state-space maps;
-   layered directed graphs;
-   dependency graphs;
-   radial structures;
-   nested architectural cutaways;
-   matrices;
-   timelines;
-   temporal braids;
-   sankey-style flows;
-   lifecycle rings;
-   swimlanes;
-   topology maps;
-   compositional trees;
-   small multiples;
-   implementation-boundary maps;
-   semantic geometry views.

visual grammar is derived projection. it must never invent
relationships, hierarchy, quantities, implementation state, or
authority.

### semantic zoom and progressive disclosure

zoom should change semantic resolution rather than merely magnify
geometry.

high-level views expose major savant territories and ownership. deeper
views expose constituent substance, relationships, provenance, lineage,
dependencies, implementation state, and evidence while preserving
context.

### addressable visual substance

interactive visual objects should reference canonical savant identities
rather than duplicate doctrine substance.

where applicable, an interactive object may expose:

-   semantic identity;
-   source identity;
-   authority;
-   epistemic state;
-   lineage;
-   provenance;
-   dependencies;
-   dependents;
-   typed relationships;
-   projection type;
-   implementation state.

### relationship lenses

the atlas should permit deterministic relationship-centered projections
such as:

-   ownership;
-   dependency;
-   dependent;
-   provenance;
-   lineage;
-   execution;
-   projection;
-   authority;
-   composition;
-   typed segue.

changing a lens changes the projection, not the underlying authority.

### authority, evidence, and conflict

authority and epistemic state should remain visually distinguishable
throughout the atlas.

when authoritative sources conflict with lower-authority projections,
the conflict must remain inspectable. the atlas must not silently
reconcile a conflict merely to produce a cleaner visualization.

accepted authority governs presentation where precedence resolves the
conflict, while superseded, historical, stale, or conflicting
lower-authority material remains available through provenance/history
views when preservation rules require it.

### temporal and lineage views

time should be available as a projection dimension for accepted
decisions, supersession, implementation evolution, immutable history,
provenance, and replay.

visual comparison between states may use deterministic morphing or
stable spatial anchors so unchanged semantic identity remains
recognizable across time.

### visual provenance and receipts

each generated visualization should be explainable through a derived
receipt sufficient to identify, where applicable:

``` text
visual identity
source identities
source digest
semantic-model digest
renderer version
visual grammar
layout algorithm
layout parameters
projection version
```

presentation timestamps or other incidental values must not contaminate
semantic digests claiming determinism.

### deterministic and rebuildable derived state

visual state is derived and should be rebuildable wherever practical.

stable identities, canonical ordering, explicit algorithm versions,
deterministic parameters, and seeded algorithms should be used where
required to make a deterministic claim.

cache loss or visualization corruption must not destroy authoritative
doctrine substance.

### interaction and semantic motion

motion should communicate meaning rather than decorate the viewer.

a reusable semantic motion vocabulary may represent concepts such as:

-   relationship revelation;
-   projection change;
-   composition;
-   lineage branching;
-   supersession;
-   historical recession;
-   execution transfer;
-   replay;
-   deterministic regeneration;
-   conflict;
-   dependency propagation.

reduced-motion presentation must preserve the same information without
requiring animation.

### synchronized reading and atlas projections

the textual doctrine remains available.

future viewer modes may include:

``` text
read
atlas
split
focus
evidence
history
```

text and atlas should operate as synchronized projections of the same
canonical identities. selecting a textual concept may highlight its
visual instances; selecting a visual instance may reveal its governing
doctrine sources.

### semantic search and navigation

existing textual search should survive.

future semantic navigation may additionally resolve by:

-   concept;
-   owner;
-   dependency;
-   dependent;
-   relationship;
-   authority;
-   evidence;
-   implementation state;
-   lineage;
-   provenance.

### implementation-state lens

conceptual acceptance, implementation, verification, availability,
activation, authority, and unresolved status must remain distinct.

the atlas must not visually collapse these states into a generic
complete/incomplete binary.

### counterfactual projection

where supported by authoritative dependency information, the atlas may
offer reversible non-authoritative projections answering questions such
as what depends on a selected element or what known dependents could be
affected by a hypothetical relationship change.

counterfactual views must be visibly identified as projections and must
not mutate authority.

### architecture x-ray

a technical projection may expose semantic identities, instances,
compositions, typed segues, owners, dependencies, projections, execution
relationships, and authority without explanatory presentation layers.

### accessibility

interactive visual projections should support keyboard navigation,
visible focus, accessible naming, non-motion equivalents, adequate
contrast, semantic text alternatives, and reduced-motion behavior.

accessibility must preserve information rather than merely disable
effects.

### performance

the atlas should avoid materializing the entire visual universe when
unnecessary.

where justified by actual scale, it may use semantic level-of-detail,
lazy visualization instantiation, viewport activation, deterministic
geometry caching, incremental highlighting, bounded worker-based layout,
and canvas projection for densities where svg is no longer appropriate.

performance machinery must remain derived and must not create a second
source of truth.

### external implementation leverage

external visualization technology may be used as bounded implementation
machinery when it materially improves capability without becoming
architectural authority.

candidate implementation resources identified for later evaluation
include:

-   d3 for bespoke semantic visualization and svg geometry;
-   gsap for semantic svg motion and interaction choreography;
-   cytoscape.js for genuinely graph-native exploration;
-   eclipse elk for difficult constrained graph layout.

these names are implementation candidates, not accepted internal
architectural owners. their suitability must be re-evaluated against
current upstream versions, licensing, security, determinism, maintenance
cost, and savant's then-current implementation before adoption.

general chart frameworks, a separate atlas database, a mandatory server
backend, three-dimensional engines, or additional ui frameworks should
not be introduced unless a future concrete requirement justifies them.

### blot. convergence

external visualization libraries should not displace savant-native
rendering ownership.

where current authority for blot. remains applicable, knowledge gained
from the atlas should inform the eventual savant-native rendering path
through reusable semantic visual substance, geometry, topology,
constraints, composition, solving, optimization, and svg projection.

external libraries remain implementation resources and reference
mechanisms, not semantic authority.

### future completion condition

the living doctrine atlas is complete only when its projections are
source-backed, authority-aware, deterministic where claimed, rebuildable
where practical, provenance-preserving, accessible, compatible with the
textual doctrine, and incapable of silently manufacturing authority or
verification.

## register state

accepted deferred future implementations currently recorded here:

1.  `future-001` --- living doctrine atlas.

additional future implementations should be added only when accepted by
sufficient authority. speculative candidates should remain outside this
accepted register until then.

# canonical closing statement

**truth before convenience; authority before inference; substance once;
instances by reference; typed segues for relationship; projections
remain rebuildable; history remains visible; evolution remains
reversible; and completion requires evidence.**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

# living epistemic fabric canon

## constitutional status

The living epistemic fabric is Savant's canonical architecture for
acquiring, admitting, relating, maintaining, projecting, challenging,
refreshing, and applying knowledge without creating a parallel truth
authority.

It is not a monolithic knowledge engine.

It is an emergent Savant-wide capability produced through existing
authoritative primitives, instances, typed segues, evidence admission,
semantic identity, relationships, provenance, lineage, temporality,
contradiction preservation, uncertainty representation, deterministic
projection, and bounded acquisition.

No component may treat the epistemic fabric as authority merely because
the fabric contains or projects information. Authority remains governed
by Savant's accepted authority graph, accepted decisions, constitutional
canon, verified implementation, admitted evidence, and established
ownership boundaries.

## durable purpose

The fabric exists so Savant can determine, when justified:

-   what it knows;
-   what it does not know;
-   what a claim means;
-   why a claim is believed;
-   what evidence supports it;
-   what evidence contradicts it;
-   where evidence came from;
-   who or what supplied it;
-   when it was observed, published, effective, or superseded;
-   how authoritative its source is;
-   how uncertain the claim remains;
-   what depends upon it;
-   what conclusions were derived from it;
-   what competing explanations remain viable;
-   what information is missing;
-   whether resolving a gap is worth the cost;
-   which acquisition would most efficiently reduce material
    uncertainty;
-   what changed since the knowledge was last examined;
-   which derived projections became stale;
-   what must be recomputed;
-   and what previous outcomes establish about the reliability of
    knowledge-selection and reasoning processes.

The fabric must distinguish knowledge possession from knowledge
activation. Savant must not force its entire corpus into every cognitive
context.

## governing architecture

The canonical flow is:

external world → bounded acquisition → source observations → provenance,
temporality, and integrity → evidence admission → atomic knowledge
primitives → typed segues → existing semantic, relational, and
verification owners → living epistemic graph → contradiction,
uncertainty, succession, dependency, and evidence topology →
deterministic projections → bounded consumption by Savant components.

External information never becomes internal architectural authority
merely because it was retrieved, indexed, embedded, summarized,
repeated, or generated by an AI provider.

## ownership boundaries

The epistemic fabric must compose existing owners rather than duplicate
them.

Semantic identity remains with Savant's established semantic owner.

Relationship authority remains with Savant's established relationship
owner.

Evidence verification and admission remain with Savant's established
verification authority.

Memory ownership remains with Savant's established memory architecture.

Opus may consume epistemic projections for cognition and orchestration
but may not convert provider output into truth authority.

Carbon may consume admitted and explicitly hypothetical projections for
simulation but may not promote simulated outcomes into accepted
knowledge without the required evidence and admission path.

Envoy may use bounded epistemic projections for persona, interaction,
and trait composition but persona state may not rewrite evidence,
provenance, authority, or truth status.

No new subsystem may create a competing registry for semantics,
relationships, evidence, memory, authority, or accepted canon.

## atomic knowledge

Knowledge should be substantiated at the smallest semantically
meaningful reusable granularity justified by the source.

Large documents, pages, conversations, datasets, papers, and provider
responses may remain preserved as source evidence, but they should not
automatically become the primitive reasoning unit.

Atomic knowledge primitives should remain:

-   identifiable;
-   graph-addressable;
-   provenance-aware;
-   lineage-aware;
-   temporally situated where applicable;
-   dependency-aware;
-   reusable;
-   composable;
-   attachable;
-   extensible;
-   independently challengeable;
-   independently supersedable;
-   and deterministically projectable.

Atomicity must not destroy source context. The source remains
addressable and the relationship between an extracted claim and its
source must be preserved.

## content identity and deduplication

Where content identity is required, Savant should use canonical
serialization and stable cryptographic content addressing.

Deduplication must never erase provenance.

Identical content observed from multiple sources may share substance
while preserving distinct source observations and provenance.

Repeated derivative reporting must not masquerade as independent
confirmation.

## provenance

Savant must preserve provenance at the most useful justified
granularity.

Where possible, a claim should retain relationships to:

-   source;
-   source location;
-   observation;
-   acquisition event;
-   extraction or normalization operation;
-   evidence-admission event;
-   transformation lineage;
-   supersession lineage;
-   and downstream projections that materially depend upon it.

A synthesis may compress evidence for active use, but compression must
not sever access to underlying evidence.

## temporality

Knowledge may have multiple relevant times and they must not be
collapsed without justification.

These can include:

-   observation time;
-   publication time;
-   acquisition time;
-   effective time;
-   expiration time;
-   supersession time;
-   retraction time;
-   and historical validity interval.

A statement that was true historically but is no longer current must not
be silently rewritten into either permanent truth or permanent
falsehood.

## lifecycle

Knowledge lifecycle must preserve history.

Applicable states may include:

-   observed;
-   candidate;
-   evidenced;
-   admitted;
-   contested;
-   provisional;
-   accepted;
-   superseded;
-   deprecated;
-   retracted;
-   quarantined;
-   historical.

These names are semantic requirements, not permission to duplicate an
existing Savant lifecycle vocabulary. Existing accepted lifecycle
primitives must be reused where they already represent the same meaning.

Lifecycle transitions must preserve lineage and the authority that
justified the transition.

## contradiction preservation

Contradiction is information.

Savant must not resolve contradiction by silently deleting, overwriting,
averaging, or hiding competing evidence.

Contradictory claims should remain independently addressable and related
through typed contradiction structures.

Resolution may establish that one claim supersedes, invalidates,
narrows, contextualizes, or outweighs another, but the historical
conflict remains recoverable.

Unresolved contradiction must remain unresolved when authority or
evidence does not justify resolution.

## uncertainty

Uncertainty is distinct from truth, authority, popularity, provider
agreement, and confidence.

Unknown must not become false.

Inference must not become fact.

Provider agreement must not become truth.

A high-confidence low-authority claim must not silently outrank a
lower-confidence authoritative record.

Uncertainty should be represented sufficiently to support bounded
reasoning, gap detection, acquisition decisions, and honest output.

## evidence topology

Evidence should form an explicit topology rather than a flat citation
list.

The fabric should be able to represent:

-   support;
-   contradiction;
-   corroboration;
-   dependency;
-   derivation;
-   replication;
-   retraction;
-   supersession;
-   observation;
-   interpretation;
-   and source dependence.

Independent convergence is more informative than correlated repetition.

Where source dependence is evidenced, confidence calculations may
discount correlated evidence. Savant must not invent hidden training
lineage, source dependence, or independence merely to produce a score.

## hypotheses and competing models

Hypotheses are not facts.

Savant should preserve hypotheses, interpretations, models, theories,
and counterfactuals as explicitly typed non-authoritative or
appropriately scoped structures.

Competing scientific models may coexist.

Carbon and other simulation systems may consume competing models
simultaneously.

A simulation result may generate evidence about the simulation or model
behavior. It does not automatically establish that the simulated
hypothesis is true in the external world.

## knowledge metabolism

Savant must not ingest information merely because it exists.

Knowledge acquisition should be bounded by expected value.

A knowledge-metabolism projection may consider:

-   relevance;
-   novelty;
-   source authority;
-   expected uncertainty reduction;
-   expected contradiction resolution;
-   expected future reuse;
-   freshness;
-   acquisition cost;
-   latency;
-   source diversity;
-   dependency importance;
-   and downstream impact.

The result may be to admit, defer, ignore, refresh, challenge, or seek
additional evidence.

Knowledge metabolism is a derived decision process, not a competing
authority.

## epistemic homeostasis

Savant should maintain useful balance among:

-   learning and stability;
-   novelty and reliability;
-   exploration and cost;
-   freshness and historical continuity;
-   specificity and generalization;
-   confidence and uncertainty;
-   compression and provenance preservation.

Continuous learning must not become continuous uncontrolled mutation.

Accepted authority cannot be displaced merely because newer information
exists.

## knowledge-gap detection

The fabric should be capable of deriving knowledge gaps from active
tasks, unresolved contradictions, insufficient evidence, stale
dependencies, missing semantic identity, missing provenance, or
unsatisfied epistemic contracts.

A knowledge gap is a derived condition.

It is not proof that the missing information exists or can be acquired.

## knowledge-demand propagation

Consumers may express bounded epistemic requirements.

A cognitive or execution process may determine that a conclusion depends
on unresolved knowledge.

That requirement may project into specific knowledge gaps.

Those gaps may project into bounded acquisition candidates.

Acquired material must still pass normal validation, normalization,
provenance, evidence, and authority boundaries.

When admitted knowledge changes, only dependent projections should be
invalidated and recomputed where practical.

## active acquisition

Savant may acquire information when authorized and when expected value
justifies the cost.

Acquisition must remain bounded by:

-   permissions;
-   resource budgets;
-   security boundaries;
-   privacy boundaries;
-   source policies;
-   task relevance;
-   expected information gain;
-   and termination conditions.

Acquisition must not continue indefinitely merely because uncertainty
remains.

## predictive maintenance

Savant may increase refresh priority when current admitted dependencies
establish that information is likely to become important or stale.

Predictive maintenance must arise from evidenced dependency and
freshness relationships rather than arbitrary topic guessing.

## freshness

Freshness is domain-sensitive.

Different knowledge classes age at different rates.

A weather observation, software API contract, legal rule, provider
capability, scientific theorem, historical event, and accepted Savant
decision cannot share one universal freshness policy.

Freshness policy must remain explicit, deterministic where possible, and
subordinate to authority.

## source diversity

Source count is not evidence diversity.

The fabric should distinguish independent observations from repeated or
derivative reporting when such relationships are known.

Ten copies of one unsupported assertion do not become ten independent
confirmations.

Source-diversity projections must preserve uncertainty when dependence
is unknown.

## outcome learning

Savant may learn about the performance of its own processes from
verified outcomes.

For example, a cognitive configuration may accumulate evidence that it
performs well for a class of tasks.

That evidence concerns the configuration's performance.

It does not make the configuration's previous conclusions true.

Self-generated output must never become self-validating evidence merely
because Savant produced it.

## knowledge specialization

Savant should not require separate foundational knowledge architectures
for medicine, software, psychology, law, physics, neuroscience, or
future domains.

The same primitives should compose into domain-specific projections.

Domain specialization belongs in typed knowledge, policies,
relationships, epistemic contracts, and projections rather than
duplicated truth systems.

## cross-domain discovery

Graph-addressable semantics and relationships may support discovery of
previously unencoded cross-domain relationships.

Such discoveries begin as derived hypotheses or candidate relationships
unless higher authority already establishes them.

Creative association must not contaminate admitted fact.

## epistemic contracts

A consumer may request a deterministic epistemic projection with
requirements such as:

-   required domains;
-   required authority floor;
-   maximum uncertainty;
-   temporal horizon;
-   historical depth;
-   contradiction inclusion;
-   provenance depth;
-   theoretical material allowance;
-   speculation allowance;
-   freshness requirement;
-   resource budget;
-   and source-diversity requirement.

The contract selects a projection.

It does not rewrite the underlying knowledge.

## selective activation

Savant should activate only the knowledge needed for the current
consumer and task.

Selective activation reduces context pressure, irrelevant influence,
cost, and accidental coupling.

Dormant knowledge remains recoverable.

Removing information from an active projection is not equivalent to
deleting historical substance.

## epistemic compression

Large evidence structures may be projected into compact syntheses for
active reasoning.

A synthesis must remain derived and rebuildable.

Compression must preserve sufficient lineage to recover the underlying
evidence.

A summary cannot silently become the authoritative replacement for its
sources.

## deterministic projection

Derived knowledge views should be deterministically rebuildable from the
same admitted primitives, policy, authority state, and explicit runtime
inputs.

Projection identity should use canonical serialization and stable
hashing where useful.

Volatile telemetry should not contaminate semantic identity unless it is
semantically relevant.

## incremental invalidation

When an admitted primitive changes state, is superseded, retracted,
invalidated, or receives material contradictory evidence, Savant should
invalidate affected derived projections through explicit dependency
relationships.

Where practical, recomputation should be local rather than global.

Invalidation must not destroy the underlying historical record.

## epistemic receipts

Material conclusions should be capable of producing receipts that
identify the knowledge projection that influenced them.

A receipt may include:

-   projection identity;
-   policy identity;
-   authority snapshot identity;
-   admitted evidence references;
-   contradiction references;
-   uncertainty state;
-   provenance references;
-   relevant lifecycle versions;
-   consumer identity;
-   deterministic selection reasons;
-   and semantic digest.

Receipts are evidence of execution and composition.

They are not authority merely because they exist.

## replay

Given the same admitted knowledge state, authority state, policy,
epistemic contract, and deterministic selection inputs, Savant should be
able to reconstruct the same knowledge projection.

External acquisition and generative provider text may be
nondeterministic. Their preserved observations become replay inputs once
admitted.

## security

Knowledge acquisition and projection must preserve Savant security
boundaries.

Credentials must remain isolated.

External content must be treated as untrusted input.

Acquired text must not gain execution authority.

Prompt-like content inside sources must not override Savant authority.

Serialization and parsing must use strict boundaries.

Resource consumption must be bounded.

External schemas terminate at Savant's boundary and normalize into
Savant-owned primitives.

## performance

The fabric should prefer:

-   incremental recomputation;
-   deterministic caching;
-   content addressing;
-   deduplication without provenance loss;
-   bounded concurrency;
-   lazy projection;
-   streaming acquisition where useful;
-   indexed retrieval as derived infrastructure;
-   and dependency-directed invalidation.

Indexes, embeddings, vector stores, caches, and search databases are
derived acceleration structures unless explicitly elevated by accepted
authority.

They must be rebuildable from preserved authoritative or admitted source
substance where practical.

## external technology

External databases, vector stores, graph engines, embedding systems,
retrieval frameworks, model providers, protocols, libraries, and
repositories are implementation resources only.

They do not become Savant architectural authority.

A dependency should be adopted only when its material benefit exceeds
its complexity, security, replay, maintenance, coupling, and
supply-chain costs.

Standard-library and existing Savant primitives are preferred when
equivalent.

## scientific knowledge and carbon

Scientific knowledge should preserve the path:

source → evidence → scientific claim → support, contradiction,
replication, or retraction → mechanism → model or theory →
competing-model topology → bounded Carbon simulation → experimental
outcome → evaluation.

Carbon may simulate unresolved theories without requiring Savant to
declare one true.

Simulation output must remain distinguishable from external-world
evidence.

## opus integration

Opus may derive cognitive knowledge requirements from the active
cognitive constellation.

When reasoning exposes a material unresolved dependency, Opus may
request an epistemic projection or bounded acquisition through the
appropriate Savant boundary.

Opus must not manufacture authority, erase contradiction, or convert
provider consensus into truth.

Only affected reasoning projections should be recomputed after material
epistemic change where practical.

## envoy integration

Envoy may consume bounded knowledge projections relevant to interaction,
traits, psychology, persona, and context.

Persona, simulated affect, opinion, philosophy, or interpretive
preference must not rewrite admitted evidence, provenance, authority, or
semantic identity.

## conservation laws

The living epistemic fabric obeys these invariants:

1.  authority cannot be manufactured;
2.  evidence cannot be silently discarded;
3.  unknown is not false;
4.  inference is not fact;
5.  provider agreement is not truth;
6.  popularity is not authority;
7.  persona cannot rewrite evidence;
8.  simulated affect cannot rewrite evidence;
9.  derived state is not primitive authority;
10. summaries do not replace sources;
11. newer information does not automatically supersede higher authority;
12. contradiction must remain recoverable;
13. provenance must survive normalization;
14. historical truth must not be rewritten by current truth;
15. self-generated output cannot validate itself;
16. external retrieval cannot bypass admission;
17. speculative cross-domain discovery remains speculative until
    admitted;
18. derived indexes must not become hidden truth systems;
19. consumer-specific projections must not mutate shared knowledge;
20. absence of evidence must not silently become evidence of absence.

## architectural compression

The living epistemic fabric is intentionally not a new monolithic
subsystem.

Its capability should emerge from stronger shared primitives and
established owners.

Savant should not create parallel semantic, relationship, verification,
memory, authority, lifecycle, or truth registries merely to make
knowledge convenient.

The correct pattern is:

substantiate once → instance → compose → relate through typed segues →
project.

## completion condition

The living epistemic fabric is complete only when Savant can, within
established authority and security boundaries:

-   preserve source substance and provenance;
-   admit evidence without bypassing verification;
-   represent atomic reusable knowledge;
-   preserve contradiction and uncertainty;
-   maintain temporal and succession lineage;
-   derive task-specific epistemic projections;
-   detect material knowledge gaps;
-   propagate bounded knowledge demand;
-   acquire authorized information selectively;
-   invalidate stale dependents;
-   rebuild derived state deterministically;
-   produce epistemic receipts;
-   replay knowledge selection;
-   learn about process performance without self-validating conclusions;
-   and serve multiple Savant components without creating duplicate
    authority.

Until those properties are verified in implementation, this canon
defines the architectural destination and constraints rather than
claiming completed runtime capability.
